=== 390 px · 1. The seller chooses immediate delivery, sees the exact money, funds, and gets one link

--- 01-form-immediate (01-form-immediate-390.jpg)
    otc.fam
    Local test chain
    Make a deal v2

    Sell tokens to one buyer. Your tokens are held by the deal contract until the buyer pays; each side pays a 0.5% fee at settlement, and nothing before.

    Delivery
    Immediate
    The buyer gets the tokens when they pay.
    Vesting
    The buyer gets a new grant that unlocks over time.
    Existing position
    A stream or a locked allocation you already hold.
    You sell
    FAM
    Token

    FAM, 18 decimals

    ↓
    Price the buyer pays you, before fees
    USDC
    Token

    USDC, 6 decimals

    Buyer

    Only this address can settle the deal, and a vesting grant only ever pays this address.

    Open for (hours)

    The buyer can settle until Wed, 25 Nov 2026, 20:01:39 UTC. After that you take the tokens back.

    Anyone can make a token with any name. Compare both token contract addresses with the ones you agreed on before you approve anything. A token that keeps a fee on its transfers is refused.

    What this deal does
    DELIVERY
    Immediate: 1,000,000 FAM go to the buyer when they pay
    BUYER, THE ONLY ADDRESS THAT CAN SETTLE
    0x5e6f708192a3b4c5d6e7f8091a2b3c4d5e6f7081
    BUYER RECEIVES
    1,000,000 FAM
    0x42c9458357f1fccd4cddcd1916d0857c1a62d2b7
    CAN BE SETTLED UNTIL
    Wed, 25 Nov 2026, 20:01:39 UTC
    PRICE
    123.456789 USDC
    BUYER FEE, 0.5% OF THE PRICE
    0.617283 USDC
    BUYER PAYS IN TOTAL
    124.074072 USDC
    SELLER FEE, 0.5% OF THE PRICE
    0.617283 USDC
    YOU RECEIVE
    122.839506 USDC
    TREASURY RECEIVES BOTH FEES
    1.234566 USDC
    0x7ea5007ea5007ea5007ea5007ea5007ea5007ea5

    Fees are rounded down to the payment token's smallest unit and taken only when the buyer settles: funding, cancelling and claiming cost no fee.

    Connect
    Approve FAM
    Fund
    Connect wallet

    Connect the wallet that holds the tokens you sell. Nothing is sent yet.

    v2 deal contract on Local test chain (0.5% fee each side, vesting): 0x89eb5891179fb8c2dbd1bb2d7167306aab3a7cd3

    v1 deal contract (no fee): 0xd07554f63fc661351b4976fcc9bf5401f6c388bf
    [in the form: delivery-immediate chosen; allocation = 1000000; base-token = 0x42c9458357f1fccd4cddcd1916d0857c1a62d2b7; quote = 123.456789; quote-token = 0x8246b2b8b128ab7744967f603359206c66e99e60; buyer = 0x5e6f708192a3b4c5d6e7f8091a2b3c4d5e6f7081; expiry-hours = 24]

--- 02-funded-link (02-funded-link-390.jpg)
    otc.fam
    Local test chain
    0x1a2b…3c4d
    Deal #1 is funded v2

    Send this one link to the buyer. It shows them the terms, both fees and that your tokens are held on the chain, and lets them settle from their wallet.

    http://127.0.0.1:38011/#/v2/d/1
    Copy link
    Open the deal
    DELIVERY
    Immediate: to the buyer's wallet
    BUYER
    0x5e6f708192a3b4c5d6e7f8091a2b3c4d5e6f7081
    THEY GET
    1,000,000 FAM
    THEY PAY, FEE INCLUDED
    124.074072 USDC
    YOU RECEIVE, FEE TAKEN
    122.839506 USDC
    CAN BE SETTLED UNTIL
    Wed, 25 Nov 2026, 20:01:40 UTC

    Your tokens are held by the deal contract. To take them back before the buyer settles, open the deal with this wallet and cancel it: that costs no fee.

    v2 deal contract on Local test chain (0.5% fee each side, vesting): 0x89eb5891179fb8c2dbd1bb2d7167306aab3a7cd3

    v1 deal contract (no fee): 0xd07554f63fc661351b4976fcc9bf5401f6c388bf

    BALANCES, funding the deal (no fee): seller -1,000,000 FAM, deals +1,000,000 FAM. Nothing else moved.

=== 390 px · 2. The seller chooses vesting delivery: a 30-day cliff over 90 days, from the settlement

--- 03-form-vesting (03-form-vesting-390.jpg)
    otc.fam
    Local test chain
    Make a deal v2

    Sell tokens to one buyer. Your tokens are held by the deal contract until the buyer pays; each side pays a 0.5% fee at settlement, and nothing before.

    Delivery
    Immediate
    The buyer gets the tokens when they pay.
    Vesting
    The buyer gets a new grant that unlocks over time.
    Existing position
    A stream or a locked allocation you already hold.
    You sell
    FAM
    Token

    FAM, 18 decimals

    ↓
    Price the buyer pays you, before fees
    USDC
    Token

    USDC, 6 decimals

    Buyer

    Only this address can settle the deal, and a vesting grant only ever pays this address.

    Open for (hours)

    The buyer can settle until Wed, 25 Nov 2026, 20:01:40 UTC. After that you take the tokens back.

    Vesting starts
    At settlement
    When the buyer pays, at that block's time.
    At a fixed time
    A date and time in UTC, still in the future.
    Cliff (days)

    30 days of chain time: nothing can be claimed before it.

    Whole vesting (days)

    90 days of chain time, from the start to fully vested.

    Anyone can make a token with any name. Compare both token contract addresses with the ones you agreed on before you approve anything. A token that keeps a fee on its transfers is refused.

    What this deal does
    DELIVERY
    Vesting: 1,000,000 FAM go into a new grant for the buyer when they pay
    BUYER, THE ONLY ADDRESS THAT CAN SETTLE
    0x5e6f708192a3b4c5d6e7f8091a2b3c4d5e6f7081
    BUYER RECEIVES
    1,000,000 FAM
    0x42c9458357f1fccd4cddcd1916d0857c1a62d2b7
    CAN BE SETTLED UNTIL
    Wed, 25 Nov 2026, 20:01:40 UTC
    PRICE
    123.456789 USDC
    BUYER FEE, 0.5% OF THE PRICE
    0.617283 USDC
    BUYER PAYS IN TOTAL
    124.074072 USDC
    SELLER FEE, 0.5% OF THE PRICE
    0.617283 USDC
    YOU RECEIVE
    122.839506 USDC
    TREASURY RECEIVES BOTH FEES
    1.234566 USDC
    0x7ea5007ea5007ea5007ea5007ea5007ea5007ea5
    VESTING SCHEDULE
    Starts when the buyer settles. Cliff 30 days later: 333,333.333333333333333333 FAM unlock at once. Fully vested 90 days after the start; until then it unlocks second by second.

    Fees are rounded down to the payment token's smallest unit and taken only when the buyer settles: funding, cancelling and claiming cost no fee.

    Connect
    Approve FAM
    Fund
    Connect wallet

    Connect the wallet that holds the tokens you sell. Nothing is sent yet.

    v2 deal contract on Local test chain (0.5% fee each side, vesting): 0x89eb5891179fb8c2dbd1bb2d7167306aab3a7cd3

    v1 deal contract (no fee): 0xd07554f63fc661351b4976fcc9bf5401f6c388bf
    [in the form: delivery-vesting chosen; allocation = 1000000; base-token = 0x42c9458357f1fccd4cddcd1916d0857c1a62d2b7; quote = 123.456789; quote-token = 0x8246b2b8b128ab7744967f603359206c66e99e60; buyer = 0x5e6f708192a3b4c5d6e7f8091a2b3c4d5e6f7081; expiry-hours = 24; start-settlement chosen; cliff-days = 30; duration-days = 90]

=== 390 px · 3. An existing vesting position is refused, before the wallet is asked for anything

--- 04-existing-position-refused (04-existing-position-refused-390.jpg)
    otc.fam
    Local test chain
    Make a deal v2

    Sell tokens to one buyer. Your tokens are held by the deal contract until the buyer pays; each side pays a 0.5% fee at settlement, and nothing before.

    Delivery
    Immediate
    The buyer gets the tokens when they pay.
    Vesting
    The buyer gets a new grant that unlocks over time.
    Existing position
    A stream or a locked allocation you already hold.
    The position you would like to sell
    What this deal does

    Nothing: this kind of delivery is not supported.

    Existing positions are not supported

    otc.fam cannot sell an existing vesting position. A Sablier stream, an allocation locked at another contract, a grant that can be revoked, a screenshot or a link: the page cannot check any of them and the deal contract cannot hold them, so none is accepted as delivery. Nothing was asked of your wallet. To sell tokens you hold, choose immediate or vesting delivery: the deal contract then holds the tokens themselves.

    v2 deal contract on Local test chain (0.5% fee each side, vesting): 0x89eb5891179fb8c2dbd1bb2d7167306aab3a7cd3

    v1 deal contract (no fee): 0xd07554f63fc661351b4976fcc9bf5401f6c388bf
    [in the form: delivery-existing chosen; existing-position = https://app.sablier.com/stream/LL2-1-1234]

    WALLET: the page only asked which account and which network; it never asked to connect, approve or send.

=== 390 px · 5. The buyer connects and settles the immediate deal: each press, then the receipt
    press 1: Connect wallet
    press 2: Approve 124.074072 USDC
    press 3: Settle: pay 124.074072 USDC
    press 4: null

=== 1440 px · 2. The seller chooses vesting delivery: a 30-day cliff over 90 days, from the settlement

--- 03-form-vesting (03-form-vesting-1440.jpg)
    otc.fam
    Local test chain
    Make a deal v2

    Sell tokens to one buyer. Your tokens are held by the deal contract until the buyer pays; each side pays a 0.5% fee at settlement, and nothing before.

    The buyer gets the tokens when they pay.

    You sell
    FAM
    Token

    FAM, 18 decimals

    ↓
    Price the buyer pays you, before fees
    USDC
    Token

    USDC, 6 decimals

    Buyer

    Only this address can settle the deal, and a vesting grant only ever pays this address.

    Open for (hours)

    The buyer can settle until Wed, 25 Nov 2026, 20:01:55 UTC. After that you take the tokens back.

    Vesting starts
    At settlement
    When the buyer pays, at that block's time.
    At a fixed time
    A date and time in UTC, still in the future.
    Cliff (days)

    30 days of chain time: nothing can be claimed before it.

    Whole vesting (days)

    90 days of chain time, from the start to fully vested.

    Anyone can make a token with any name. Compare both token contract addresses with the ones you agreed on before you approve anything. A token that keeps a fee on its transfers is refused.

    DELIVERY
    Vesting: 1,000,000 FAM go into a new grant for the buyer when they pay

    1.234566 USDC

    VESTING SCHEDULE
    Starts when the buyer settles. Cliff 30 days later: 333,333.333333333333333333 FAM unlock at once. Fully vested 90 days after the start; until then it unlocks second by second.

=== 1440 px · 3. An existing vesting position is refused, before the wallet is asked for anything

--- 04-existing-position-refused (04-existing-position-refused-1440.jpg)
    otc.fam
    Local test chain
    Make a deal v2

    Sell tokens to one buyer. Your tokens are held by the deal contract until the buyer pays; each side pays a 0.5% fee at settlement, and nothing before.

    Delivery
    Immediate
    The buyer gets the tokens when they pay.
    Vesting
    The buyer gets a new grant that unlocks over time.
    Existing position
    A stream or a locked allocation you already hold.
    The position you would like to sell
    What this deal does

    Nothing: this kind of delivery is not supported.

    Existing positions are not supported

    otc.fam cannot sell an existing vesting position. A Sablier stream, an allocation locked at another contract, a grant that can be revoked, a screenshot or a link: the page cannot check any of them and the deal contract cannot hold them, so none is accepted as delivery. Nothing was asked of your wallet. To sell tokens you hold, choose immediate or vesting delivery: the deal contract then holds the tokens themselves.

    v2 deal contract on Local test chain (0.5% fee each side, vesting): 0x89eb5891179fb8c2dbd1bb2d7167306aab3a7cd3

    v1 deal contract (no fee): 0xd07554f63fc661351b4976fcc9bf5401f6c388bf
    [in the form: delivery-existing chosen; existing-position = https://app.sablier.com/stream/LL2-1-1234]

    WALLET: the page only asked which account and which network; it never asked to connect, approve or send.
